Um... well, like I was saying, it’s mostly just that I keep getting up from my workstation when I really know I’m supposed to just stay put and finish my claim reviews. As for what goes through my mind... it’s honestly not a conscious choice to avoid work, I promise.

Um, well... I wouldn't say I *lose* things permanently. I mean, I always find them! It's just... I guess sometimes I misplace things for a minute or two. 

Like, I'll be working on a claim and then I'll realize I don't know where the physical form went, even though I swear I just had it in my hand. Or I'll set my headset down to grab a coffee, and then I have to spend a minute hunting for it when I get back to my desk. Sometimes it's a sticky note with a claimant's callback number, or... well, sometimes I've even misplaced my office access badge, just for a little bit! 

But I always find them pretty quickly, I promise! I just... I think I just set things down without thinking when I'm trying to juggle a bunch of different tasks. That happens to everyone, right? Just, like, temporarily misplacing things when you're busy? I really try to keep track of my stuff, I don't want you to think I'm irresponsible or anything.
